\"redirect_chain\", \"redirect_loop\"\n    ],\n    \"medium\": [\n        \"title_too_long\", \"missing_meta_description\", \"duplicate_meta_description\",\n        \"missing_h1\", \"redirect\", \"duplicate_h1\",\n        \"thin_content\", \"slow_page\"\n    ]\n}\n\ndef get_severity(issue_type):\n    for sev, issues in SEVERITY.items():\n        if issue_type in issues:\n            return sev\n    return \"low\"\n\ndef load_csv(path: str) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Loads Screaming Frog internal_all.csv into a pandas DataFrame.\"\"\"\n    return pd.read_csv(path, encoding=\"utf-8-sig\", low_memory=False)\n\ndef detect(df: pd.DataFrame) -> list:\n    \"\"\"\n    Detects SEO issues based on the provided DataFrame.\n    Returns a list of dicts with keys: url, issue_type, severity, detail.\n    \"\"\"\n    issues = []\n\n    # Standard Screaming Frog internal_all export column names\n    col_url = 'Address'\n    col_title = 'Title 1'\n    col_meta = 'Meta Description 1'\n    col_status = 'Status Code'\n    col_h1 = 'H1-1'\n    col_index = 'Indexability'\n    col_canonical = 'Canonical Link Element 1'\n    col_words = 'Word Count'\n    col_time = 'Response Time'\n    col_inlinks = 'Inlinks'\n    col_alt = 'Img Alt Text'\n    col_content_type = 'Content Type'\n    col_chain = 'Redirect Chain'\n    col_loop = 'Redirect Loop'\n\n    # Pre-calculate duplicates for efficient lookup\n    indexable_df = df[(df[col_index] == 'Indexable') & (df[col_status] == 200)] if col_index in df.columns and col_status in df.columns else df\n    titles = indexable_df[col_title].fillna('') if col_title in indexable_df.columns else pd.Series([], dtype=str)\n    metas = indexable_df[col_meta].fillna('') if col_meta in indexable_df.columns else pd.Series([], dtype=str)\n    h1s = indexable_df[col_h1].fillna('') if col_h1 in indexable_df.columns else pd.Series([], dtype=str)\n\n    dup_titles = set(titles[titles.duplicated()].unique())\n    dup_metas = set(metas[metas.duplicated()].unique())\n    dup_h1s = set(h1s[h1s.duplicated()].unique())\n\n    for _, row in df.iterrows():\n        url = row.get(col_url, \"Unknown\")\n        title = str(row.get(col_title, \"\")) if pd.notnull(row.get(col_title)) else \"\"\n        meta = str(row.get(col_meta, \"\")) if pd.notnull(row.get(col_meta)) else \"\"\n        status = row.get(col_status)\n        h1 = str(row.get(col_h1, \"\")) if pd.notnull(row.get(col_h1)) else \"\"\n        indexability = row.get(col_index)\n        canonical = str(row.get(col_canonical, \"\")) if pd.notnull(row.get(col_canonical)) else \"\"\n        words = row.get(col_words)\n        resp_time = row.get(col_time)\n        inlinks = row.get(col_inlinks)\n\n        # Rule definitions\n        checks = [\n            (\"missing_title\", (indexability == 'Indexable' and status == 200) and not title, \"Page is missing a title tag\"),\n            (\"duplicate_title\", (indexability == 'Indexable' and status == 200) and (title and title in dup_titles), f\"Duplicate title: {title}\"),\n            (\"broken_link\", isinstance(status, (int, float)) and 400 <= status < 500, f\"Broken link with status {status}\"),\n            (\"server_error\", isinstance(status, (int, float)) and status >= 500, f\"Server error with status {status}\"),\n            (\"redirect_chain\", row.get(col_chain) if col_chain in df.columns else False, \"Page is part of a redirect chain\"),\n            (\"redirect_loop\", row.get(col_loop) if col_loop in df.columns else False, \"Page is in a redirect loop\"),\n            (\"title_too_long\", len(title) > 60, f\"Title length ({len(title)}) exceeds 60 characters\"),\n            (\"missing_meta_description\", (indexability == 'Indexable' and status == 200) and not meta, \"Meta description is missing\"),\n            (\"duplicate_meta_description\", (indexability == 'Indexable' and status == 200) and (meta and meta in dup_metas), \"Duplicate meta description detected\"),\n            (\"missing_h1\", not h1, \"H1 tag is missing\"),\n            (\"redirect\", isinstance(status, (int, float)) and status in [301, 302], f\"Page is a redirect ({status})\"),\n            (\"missing_image_alt\", (str(row.get('Content Type', '')).lower().find('image') != -1) and not title, \"Image missing alt text\"),\n            (\"orphan_page\", status == 200 and isinstance(inlinks, (int, float)) and inlinks == 0, \"Page has 0 inlinks (orphan page)\"),\n            (\"non_indexable_but_linked\", status == 200 and indexability != \"Indexable\", f\"Page is non-indexable ({indexability}) but present in crawl\"),\n            (\"canonical_mismatch\", canonical and canonical != url, f\"Canonical mismatch: {canonical}\"),\n            (\"title_too_short\", title and len(title) < 30, f\"Title length ({len(title)}) is too short (below 30)\"),\n            (\"meta_description_too_long\", meta and len(meta) > 155, f\"Meta description length ({len(meta)}) exceeds 155 characters\"),\n            (\"duplicate_h1\", h1 and h1 in dup_h1s, f\"Duplicate H1 detected: {h1}\"),\n            (\"thin_content\", isinstance(words, (int, float)) and words < 200, f\"Thin content: only {words} words\"),\n            (\"slow_page\", isinstance(resp_time, (int, float)) and resp_time > 1.0, f\"Page response time is slow: {resp_time}ms\"),\n        ]\n\n        for issue_type, condition, detail in checks:\n            if condition:\n                issues.append({\n                    \"url\": url,\n dtype=str)\n60\t\n61\t    dup_titles = set(titles[titles.duplicated()].unique())\n62\t    dup_metas = set(metas[metas.duplicated()].unique())\n63\t    dup_h1s = set(h1s[h1s.duplicated()].unique())\n64\t\n65\t    for _, row in df.iterrows():\n66\t        url = row.get(col_url, \"Unknown\")\n67\t        title = str(row.get(col_title, \"\")) if definitions\n78\t        checks = [\n79\t            (\"missing_title\", (indexability == 'Indexable' and status == 200) and not title, \"Page is missing a title tag\"),\n80\t            (\"duplicate_title\", (indexability == 'Indexable' and status == 200) and (title and title in dup_titles), f\"Duplicate title: {title}\"),\n81\t            (\"broken_link\", isinstance(status, (int, {status}\"),\n83\t            (\"redirect_chain\", row.get(col_chain) if col_chain in df.columns else False, \"Page is part of a redirect chain\"),\n84\t            (\"redirect_loop\", (\"missing_meta_description\", (indexability == 'Indexable' and status == 200) and not meta, \"Meta description is missing\"),\n87\t            (\"duplicate_meta_description\", (indexability == {"parentUuid":"8d5b46a5-c299-415e-b03e-6d6e9b660f46","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- deep-research: Deep research harness — fan-out web searches, fetch sources, adversarially verify claims, synthesize a cited report. - When the user wants a deep, multi-source, fact-checked research report on any topic. BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by running the app and observing behavior. Use when asked to verify a PR, confirm a fix works, test a change manually, check that a feature works, or validate local changes before pushing.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch","skillCount":13,"isInitial":true,"names":["deep-research","update-config","keybindings-help","verify","code-review","simplify","fewer-permission-prompts","loop","claude-api","run","init","review","security-review"]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"5fa79bfd-b885-4855-9fc4-3bec4e562200","timestamp":"2026-06-06T07:19:29.540Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/AayushShukla/Desktop/seo-command-center","sessionId":"19ecbf2d-2b2d-4bc6-b05b-3e4a0cbb3762","version":"2.1.167","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"7791d7f9-08f9-4a3a-af20-23059695f7c7","isSidechain":false,"message":{"id":"msg_c63d5cd9ee48f8a10792520d","type":"message","role":"assistant","model":"gemma4:31b","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_bz7zxyg0","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/Users/AayushShukla/Desktop/seo-command-center/seo/detector.py","content":"import pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Severity Mapping from CLAUDE.md\nSEVERITY = {\n    \"high\": [\n        \"missing_title\", \"duplicate_title\", \"broken_link\",\n        \"server_error\", \"redirect_chain\", \"redirect_loop\"\n    ],\n    \"medium\": [\n        \"title_too_long\", \"missing_meta_description\", \"duplicate_meta_description\",\n        \"missing_h1\", \"redirect\", \"missing_image_alt\",\n        \"orphan_page\", \"non_indexable_but_linked\", \"canonical_mismatch\"\n    ],\n    \"low\": [\n        \"title_too_short\", \"meta_description_too_long\", \"duplicate_h1\",\n        \"thin_content\", \"slow_page\"\n    ]\n}\n\ndef get_severity(issue_type):\n    for sev, issues in SEVERITY.items():\n        if issue_type in issues:\n            return sev\n    return \"low\"\n\ndef load_csv(path):\n    \"\"\"Loads Screaming Frog internal_all.csv into a pandas DataFrame.\"\"\"\n    return pd.read_csv(path)\n\ndef detect(df):\n    \"\"\"\n    Detects SEO issues based on the provided DataFrame.\n    Returns a list of dicts with keys: url, issue_type, severity, detail.\n    \"\"\"\n    issues = []\n\n    # Column mappings based on standard Screaming Frog internal_all export\n    col_url = 'Address'\n    col_title = 'Title 1'\n    col_meta = 'Meta Description 1'\n    col_status = 'Status Code'\n    col_h1 = 'H1-1'\n    col_index = 'Indexability'\n    col_canonical = 'Canonical'\n    col_words = 'Word Count'\n    col_time = 'Response Time'\n    col_inlinks = 'Inlinks'\n    col_alt = 'Img Alt Text' # Often in a separate report, but checking if present\n    col_chain = 'Redirect Chain' # Often in a separate report\n    col_loop = 'Redirect Loop'   # Often in a separate report\n\n    # Pre-calculate duplicates\n    titles = df[col_title].fillna('')\n    metas = df[col_meta].fillna('')\n    h1s = df[col_h1].fillna('')\n\n    dup_titles = titles[titles.duplicated()].unique()\n    dup_metas = metas[metas.duplicated()].unique()\n    dup_h1s = h1s[h1s.duplicated()].unique()\n\n    for _, row in df.iterrows():\n        url = row[col_url]\n        title = str(row[col_title]) if pd.notnull(row[col_title]) else \"\"\n        meta = str(row[col_meta]) if pd.notnull(row[col_meta]) else \"\"\n        status = row[col_status]\n        h1 = str(row[col_h1]) if pd.notnull(row[col_h1]) else \"\"\n        indexability = row[col_index]\n        canonical = str(row[col_canonical]) if pd.notnull(row[col_canonical]) else \"\"\n        words = row[col_words]\n        resp_time = row[col_time]\n        inlinks = row[col_inlinks]\n\n        # 1. missing_title (High)\n        if not title:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"missing_title\", \"severity\": \"high\", \"detail\": \"Page is missing a title tag\"})\n\n        # 2. duplicate_title (High)\n        if title and title in dup_titles:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"duplicate_title\", \"severity\": \"high\", \"detail\": f\"Duplicate title: {title}\"})\n\n        # 3. broken_link (High)\n        if isinstance(status, (int, float)) and 400 <= status < 500:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"broken_link\", \"severity\": \"high\", \"detail\": f\"Broken link with status {status}\"})\n\n        # 4. server_error (High)\n        if isinstance(status, (int, float)) and status >= 500:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"server_error\", \"severity\": \"high\", \"detail\": f\"Server error with status {status}\"})\n\n        # 5. redirect_chain (High)\n        if col_chain in df.columns and row[col_chain]:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"redirect_chain\", \"severity\": \"high\", \"detail\": \"Page is part of a redirect chain\"})\n\n        # 6. redirect_loop (High)\n        if col_loop in df.columns and row[col_loop]:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"redirect_loop\", \"severity\": \"high\", \"detail\": \"Page is in a redirect loop\"})\n\n        # 7. title_too_long (Medium)\n        if len(title) > 60:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"title_too_long\", \"severity\": \"medium\", \"detail\": f\"Title length ({len(title)}) exceeds 60 characters\"})\n\n        # 8. missing_meta_description (Medium)\n        if not meta:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"missing_meta_description\", \"severity\": \"medium\", \"detail\": \"Meta description is missing\"})\n\n        # 9. duplicate_meta_description (Medium)\n        if meta and meta in dup_metas:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"duplicate_meta_description\", \"severity\": \"medium\", \"detail\": \"Duplicate meta description detected\"})\n\n        # 10. missing_h1 (Medium)\n        if not h1:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"missing_h1\", \"severity\": \"medium\", \"detail\": \"H1 tag is missing\"})\n\n        # 11. redirect (Medium)\n        if isinstance(status, (int, float)) and status in [301, 302]:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"redirect\", \"severity\": \"medium\", \"detail\": f\"Page is a redirect ({status})\"})\n\n        # 12. missing_image_alt (Medium)\n        if col_alt in df.columns and (pd.isnull(row[col_alt]) or not str(row[col_alt]).strip()):\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"missing_image_alt\", \"severity\": \"medium\", \"detail\": \"Image missing alt text\"})\n\n        # 13. orphan_page (Medium)\n        if isinstance(inlinks, (int, float)) and inlinks == 0:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"orphan_page\", \"severity\": \"medium\", \"detail\": \"Page has 0 inlinks (orphan page)\"})\n\n        # 14. non_indexable_but_linked (Medium)\n        if indexability != \"Indexable\":\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"non_indexable_but_linked\", \"severity\": \"medium\", \"detail\": f\"Page is non-indexable ({indexability}) but present in crawl\"})\n\n        # 15. canonical_mismatch (Medium)\n        if canonical and canonical != url:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"canonical_mismatch\", \"severity\": \"medium\", \"detail\": f\"Canonical mismatch: {canonical}\"})\n\n        # 16. title_too_short (Low)\n        if title and len(title) < 30:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"title_too_short\", \"severity\": \"low\", \"detail\": f\"Title length ({len(title)}) is too short (below 30)\"})\n\n        # 17. meta_description_too_long (Low)\n        if meta and len(meta) > 155:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"meta_description_too_long\", \"severity\": \"low\", \"detail\": f\"Meta description length ({len(meta)}) exceeds 155 characters\"})\n\n        # 18. duplicate_h1 (Low)\n        if h1 and h1 in dup_h1s:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"duplicate_h1\", \"severity\": \"low\", \"detail\": f\"Duplicate H1 detected: {h1}\"})\n\n        # 19. thin_content (Low)\n        if isinstance(words, (int, float)) and words < 300:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"thin_content\", \"severity\": \"low\", \"detail\": f\"Thin content: only {words} words\"})\n\n        # 20. slow_page (Low)\n        if isinstance(resp_time, (int, float)) and resp_time > 2000:\n            issues.append({\"url\": url, \"issue_type\": \"slow_page\", \"severity\": \"low\", \"detail\": f\"Page response time is slow: {resp_time}ms\"})\n\n    return issues\n"}}],"usage":{"input_tokens":21376,"cache_creation_input_tokens":0,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":3297,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"},"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_details":null},"type":"assistant","uuid":"5495c167-43dc-4733-bc9b-b85d1b9befa1","timestamp":"2026-06-06T07:20:30.101Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/AayushShukla/Desktop/seo-command-center","sessionId":"19ecbf2d-2b2d-4bc6-b05b-3e4a0cbb3762","version":"2.1.167","gitBranch":"main"}

{"parentUuid":"af9fdcce-8dc2-4c92-8be0-dad681ea31c9","isSidechain":false,"message":{"id":"msg_5b06ea05e684509ed2f1849d","type":"message","role":"assistant","model":"gemma4:31b","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_ewex3p05","name":"Edit","input":{"replace_all":false,"file_path":"/Users/Aayushukla/Desktop/seo-command-center/run.py","old_string":"","new_string":"import sys\nimport os\nimport json\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom seo.detector import load_csv, detect\n\ndef main():\n    if len(sys.argv) < 2:\n        print(\"Usage: python run.py <export_dir>\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    export_dir = sys.argv[1]\n    \n    # Stage 1: Load CSV\n    # Assuming the CSV is located in the export_dir as internal_all.csv\n    csv_path = os.path.join(export_dir, 'internal_all.csv')\n    print(f\"Stage 1: Loading CSV from {csv_path}...\")\n    try:\n        df = load_csv(csv_path)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error loading CSV: {e}\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    # Stage 2: Detect Issues\n    print(\"Stage 2: Detecting issues...\")\n    issues = detect(df)\n\n    # Stage 3: Sort and Recommendations\n    print(\"Stage 3: Prioritizing issues and generating recommendations...\")\n    # Sort by severity: High > Medium > Low\n    severity_map = {\"high\": 0, \"medium\": 1, \"low\": 2}\n    sorted_issues = sorted(issues, key=lambda x: severity_map.get(x['severity'].lower(), 3))\n    \n    # Generate top 5 recommendations based on the most frequent High/Medium issues\n    # For simplicity, we'll take a sample of high-severity issue types\n    high_issues = [i for i in sorted_issues if i['severity'].lower() == 'high']\n    rec_candidates = {}\n    for i in high_issues:\n        rec_candidates[i['issue_type']] = rec_candidates.get(i['issue_type'], 0) + 1\n    \n    top_issue_types = sorted(rec_candidates, key=rec_candidates.get, reverse=True)[:5]\n    recommendations = [f\"Prioritize fixing {ity} issues\" for ity in top_issue_types]\n    if not recommendations:\n        recommendations = [\"No high-priority issues found. Perform a general SEO health check.\"]\n\n    # Stage 4: Save outputs/report.json\n    print(\"Stage 4: Saving report...\")\n    output_dir = os.path.join(export_dir, 'outputs')\n    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    report_path = os.path.join(output_dir, 'report.json')\n    \n    # Simple summary\n    summary = {\n        \"total_issues\": len(issues),\n        \"high\": len([i for i in issues if i['severity'].lower() == 'high']),\n        \"medium\": len([i for i in issues if i['severity'].lower() == 'medium']),\n        \"low\": len([i for i in issues if i['severity'].lower() == 'low']),\n    }\n\n    report = {\n        \"site\": export_dir, # Approximation\n        \"urls_crawled\": len(df),\n        \"summary\": summary,\n        \"issues\": sorted_issues,\n        \"fixes\": [], # To be filled by fixer agent later\n        \"recommendations\": recommendations,\n        \"run_meta\": {\n            \"tool\": \"SEO Command Center\",\n            \"version\": \"1.0\"\n        }\n    }\n\n    with open(report_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=4)\n\n    # Stage 5: Print summary to terminal\n    print(\"\\n--- SEO Audit Summary ---\")\n    print(f\"URLs Crawled: {report['urls_crawled']}\")\n    print(f\"Total Issues Found: {summary['total_issues']}\")\n    print(f\"  - High: {summary['high']}\")\n    print(f\"  - Medium: {summary['medium']}\")\n    print(f\"  - Low: {summary['low']}\")\n    print(\"\\nTop Recommendations:\")\n    for rec in recommendations:\n        print(f\"- {rec}\")\n    print(f\"\\nReport saved to: {report_path}\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"}}],"usage":{"input_tokens":27689,"cache_creation_input_tokens":0,"cache_read_input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":1872,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0},"inference_geo":"","iterations":[],"speed":"standard"},"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_details":null},"type":"assistant","uuid":"fd56ed89-aeec-4407-81b3-c25db4b107b3","timestamp":"2026-06-06T07:28:28.423Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/AayushShukla/Desktop/seo-command-center","sessionId":"19ecbf2d-2b2d-4bc6-b05b-3e4a0cbb3762","version":"2.1.167","gitBranch":"main"}

(file state is current in your context — no need to Read it back)"}]},"uuid":"b443902d-1aa0-4cc2-91eb-a07c68ba0ecf","timestamp":"2026-06-06T07:31:33.386Z","toolUseResult":{"filePath":"/Users/AayushShukla/Desktop/seo-command-center/run.py","oldString":"","newString":"import sys\nimport os\nimport json\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom seo.detector import load_csv, detect\n\ndef main():\n    if len(sys.argv) < 2:\n        print(\"Usage: python run.py <export_dir>\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    export_dir = sys.argv[1]\n\n    # Stage 1: Load CSV\n    # Assuming the CSV is located in the export_dir as internal_all.csv\n    csv_path = os.path.join(export_dir, 'internal_all.csv')\n    print(f\"Stage 1: Loading CSV from {csv_path}...\")\n    try:\n        df = load_csv(csv_path)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error loading CSV: {e}\")\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n    # Stage 2: Detect Issues\n    print(\"Stage 2: Detecting issues...\")\n    issues = detect(df)\n\n    # Stage 3: Sort and Recommendations\n    print(\"Stage 3: Prioritizing issues and generating recommendations...\")\n    # Sort by severity: High > Medium > Low\n    severity_map = {\"high\": 0, \"medium\": 1, \"low\": 2}\n    sorted_issues = sorted(issues, key=lambda x: severity_map.get(x['severity'].lower(), 3))\n\n    # Generate top 5 recommendations based on the most frequent High/Medium issues\n    # For simplicity, we'll take a sample of high-severity issue types\n    high_issues = [i for i in sorted_issues if i['severity'].lower() == 'high']\n    rec_candidates = {}\n    for i in high_issues:\n        rec_candidates[i['issue_type']] = rec_candidates.get(i['issue_type'], 0) + 1\n\n    top_issue_types = sorted(rec_candidates, key=rec_candidates.get, reverse=True)[:5]\n    recommendations = [f\"Prioritize fixing {ity} issues\" for ity in top_issue_types]\n    if not recommendations:\n        recommendations = [\"No high-priority issues found. Perform a general SEO health check.\"]\n\n    # Stage 4: Save outputs/report.json\n    print(\"Stage 4: Saving report...\")\n    output_dir = os.path.join(export_dir, 'outputs')\n    os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    report_path = os.path.join(output_dir, 'report.json')\n\n    # Simple summary\n    summary = {\n        \"total_issues\": len(issues),\n        \"high\": len([i for i in issues if i['severity'].lower() == 'high']),\n        \"medium\": len([i for i in issues if i['severity'].lower() == 'medium']),\n        \"low\": len([i for i in issues if i['severity'].lower() == 'low']),\n    }\n\n    report = {\n        \"site\": export_dir, # Approximation\n        \"urls_crawled\": len(df),\n        \"summary\": summary,\n        \"issues\": sorted_issues,\n        \"fixes\": [], # To be filled by fixer agent later\n        \"recommendations\": recommendations,\n        \"run_meta\": {\n            \"tool\": \"SEO Command Center\",\n            \"version\": \"1.0\"\n        }\n    }\n\n    with open(report_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=4)\n\n    # Stage 5: Print summary to terminal\n    print(\"\\n--- SEO Audit Summary ---\")\n    print(f\"URLs Crawled: {report['urls_crawled']}\")\n    print(f\"Total Issues Found: {summary['total_issues']}\")\n    print(f\"  - High: {summary['high']}\")\n    print(f\"  - Medium: {summary['medium']}\")\n    print(f\"  - Low: {summary['low']}\")\n    print(\"\\nTop Recommendations:\")\n    for rec in recommendations:\n        print(f\"- {rec}\")\n    print(f\"\\nReport saved to: {report_path}\")\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","originalFile":"","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":1,"oldLines":0,"newStart":1,"newLines":89,"lines":["+import sys","+import os","+import json","+import pandas as pd","+from seo.detector import load_csv, detect","+","+def main():","+    if len(sys.argv) < 2:","+        print(\"Usage: python run.py <export_dir>\")","+        sys.exit(1)","+","+    export_dir = sys.argv[1]","+","+    # {"parentUuid":"4d4619f0-0fb6-4d0a-ad68-927352b55c25","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"skill_listing","content":"- deep-research: Deep research harness — fan-out web searches, fetch sources, adversarially verify claims, synthesize a cited report. - When the user wants a deep, multi-source, fact-checked research report on any topic. BEFORE invoking, check if the question is specific enough to research directly — if underspecified (e.g., \"what car to buy\" without budget/use-case/region), ask 2-3 clarifying questions to narrow scope. Then pass the refined question as args, weaving the answers in.\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by running the app and observing behavior. Use when asked to verify a PR, confirm a fix works, test a change manually, check that a feature works, or validate local changes before pushing.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch","skillCount":13,"isInitial":true,"names":["deep-research","update-config","keybindings-help","verify","code-review","simplify","fewer-permission-prompts","loop","claude-api","run","init","review","security-review"]},"type":"attachment","uuid":"50e39a07-deeb-43c4-a212-f4d3a0308812","timestamp":"2026-06-06T07:39:41.664Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/AayushShukla/Desktop/seo-command-center","sessionId":"29d97f88-2ce2-4ab7-b467-a84aae7f9c50","version":"2.1.167","gitBranch":"main"}

ensure_output_dir():\n    if not os.path.exists(OUTPUT_DIR):\n        os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR)\n\ndef to_html(report_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Writes outputs/report.html showing: site name, urls crawled, site_name = report_dict.get(\"site_name\", \"Unknown Site\")\n    urls_crawled = report_dict.get(\"urls_crawled\", 0)\n    severity_counts = report_dict.get(\"severity_counts\", {})\n    top_issues = report_dict.get(\"top_issues\", [])\n    recommendations = report_dict.get(\"recommendations\", [])\n\n    severity_html = \"\".join([f\"<li>{sev}: {count}</li>\" for sev, count in severity_counts.items()])\n    \n    issues_rows = \"\".join([\n        f\"<tr><td>{issue.get('severity', 'N/A')}</td><td>{issue.get('issue', 'N/A')}</td><td>{issue.get('url', 'N/A')}</td></tr>\"\n        for issue in top_issues\n    ])\n\n    recs_html = \"\".join([f\"<li>{rec}</li>\" for rec in recommendations])\n\n    html_content = f\"\"\"\n    <!DOCTYPE html>\n    <html lang=\"en\">\n    <head>\n        <meta charset=\"UTF-8\">\n        <title>SEO Report - {site_name}</title>\n        <style>\n            body {{ font-family: Arial, sans-serif; margin: 40px; line-height: 1.6; }}\n            h1, h2 {{ color: #333; }}\n            table {{ border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; margin-bottom: 20px; }}\n            th, td {{ border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 8px; text-align: left; }}\n            th {{ background-color: #f2f2f2; }}\n            .severity-high {{ color: red; font-weight: bold; }}\n            .severity-medium {{ color: orange; font-weight: bold; }}\n            .severity-low {{ color: green; font-weight: bold; }}\n        </style>\n    </head>\n    <body>\n        <h1>SEO Audit Report: {site_name}</h1>\n        <p><strong>URLs Crawled:</strong> {urls_crawled}</p>\n        \n        <h2>Issue Summary</h2>\n        <ul>\n            {severity_html}\n        </ul>\n\n        <h2>Top Issues</h2>\n        <table>\n            <thead>\n                <tr><th>Severity</th><th>Issue</th><th>URL</th></tr>\n            </thead>\n            <tbody>\n                {issues_rows}\n            </tbody>\n        </table>\n\n        <h2>Recommendations</h2>\n        <ul>\n            {recs_html}\n        </ul>\n    </body>\n    </html>\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    with open(output_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(html_content)\n\ndef to_pdf(report_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Writes outputs/report.pdf using fpdf2 library.\n    \"\"\"\n    ensure_output_dir()\n    output_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"report.pdf\")\n    \n    pdf = FPDF()\n    pdf.add_page()\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"B\", 16)\n    \n    site_name = report_dict.get(\"site_name\", \"Unknown Site\")\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, f\"SEO Audit Report: {site_name}\", ln=True, align=\"C\")\n    pdf.ln(10)\n    \n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"\", 12)\n    urls_crawled = report_dict.get(\"urls_crawled\", 0)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, f\"URLs Crawled: {urls_crawled}\", ln=True)\n    pdf.ln(5)\n    \n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"B\", 14)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, \"Issue Summary\", ln=True)\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"\", 12)\n    severity_counts = report_dict.get(\"severity_counts\", {})\n    for sev, count in severity_counts.items():\n        pdf.cell(0, 10, f\"{sev}: {count}\", ln=True)\n    pdf.ln(5)\n    \n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"B\", 14)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, \"Top Issues\", ln=True)\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"\", 10)\n    \n    # Simple table header\n    pdf.cell(30, 10, \"Severity\", 1)\n    pdf.cell(80, 10, \"Issue\", 1)\n    pdf.cell(80, 10, \"URL\", 1)\n    pdf.ln()\n    \n    top_issues = report_dict.get(\"top_issues\", [])\n    for issue in top_issues:\n        pdf.cell(30, 10, issue.get(\"severity\", \"N/A\"), 1)\n        pdf.cell(80, 10, issue.get(\"issue\", \"N/A\")[:75], 1) # Simple truncation\n        pdf.cell(80, 10, issue.get(\"url\", \"N/A\")[:75], 1)\n        pdf.ln()\n    pdf.ln(5)\n    \n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"B\", 14)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, \"Recommendations\", ln=True)\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"\", 12)\n    recommendations = report_dict.get(\"recommendations\", [])\n    for rec in recommendations:\n        pdf.multi_cell(0, 10, f\"- {rec}\")\n        pdf.ln(2)\n        \n    pdf.output(output_path)\n\ndef to_pptx(report_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Writes outputs/report.pptx using python-pptx library.\n    \"\"\"\n    ensure_output_dir()\n    output_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"report.pptx\")\n    \n    prs = Presentation()\n    \n    # Slide 1: Title\n    title_slide_layout = prs.slide_layouts[0]\n    slide = prs.slides.add_slide(title_slide_layout)\n    title = slide.shapes.title\n    subtitle = slide.placeholders[1]\n    site_name = report_dict.get(\"site_name\", \"Unknown Site\")\n    title.text = f\"SEO Audit Report\"\n    subtitle.text = f\"Site: {site_name}\\nGenerated on 2026-06-06\"\n\n    # Slide 2: Summary\n    summary_layout = prs.slide_layouts[1]\n    slide = prs.slides.add_slide(summary_layout)\n    slide.shapes.title.text = \"Executive Summary\"\n    tf = slide.placeholders[1].text_frame\n    urls_crawled = report_dict.get(\"urls_crawled\", 0)\n    tf.text = f\"Total URLs Crawled: {urls_crawled}\"\n    \n    severity_counts = report_dict.get(\"severity_counts\", {})\n    for sev, count in severity_counts.items():\n        p = tf.add_paragraph()\n        p.text = f\"{sev} Severity Issues: {count}\"\n\n    # Slide 3: Top Issues\n    issues_layout = prs.slide_layouts[1]\n    slide = prs.slides.add_slide(issues_layout)\n    slide.shapes.title.text = \"Top SEO Issues\"\n    tf = slide.placeholders[1].text_frame\n    top_issues = report_dict.get(\"top_issues\", [])\n    if not top_issues:\n        tf.text = \"No critical issues found.\"\n    else:\n        for issue in top_issues[:5]: # Limit to top 5 for slide readability\n            p = tf.add_paragraph()\n            p.text = f\"[{issue.get('severity')}] {issue.get('issue')}\"\n            p.level = 0\n\n    # Slide 4: Recommendations\n    recs_layout = prs.slide_layouts[1]\n    slide = prs.slides.add_slide(recs_layout)\n    slide.shapes.title.text = \"Key Recommendations\"\n    tf = slide.placeholders[1].text_frame\n    recommendations = report_dict.get(\"recommendations\", [])\n    if not recommendations:\n        tf.text = \"No specific recommendations at this time.\"\n    else:\n        for rec in recommendations[:5]: # Limit to top 5\n            p = tf.add_paragraph()\n            p.text = rec\n            p.level = 0\n\n    prs.save(output_path)\n```\n\nI'll apply this to os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR)\n\ndef to_html(report_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Writes outputs/report.html showing: site name, urls crawled,\n    issue counts by severity, top issues as an HTML table, recommendations list.\n    \"\"\"\n    ensure_output_dir()\n    output_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"report.html\")\n\n    site_name = report_dict.get(\"site_name\", \"Unknown Site\")\n    urls_crawled = report_dict.get(\"urls_crawled\", 0)\n    severity_counts = report_dict.get(\"severity_counts\", {})\n    top_issues = report_dict.get(\"top_issues\", [])\n    recommendations = report_dict.get(\"recommendations\", [])\n\n    severity_html = \"\".join([f\"<li>{sev}: {count}</li>\" for sev, count in severity_counts.items()])\n\n    issues_rows = \"\".join([\n \"\".join([f\"<li>{rec}</li>\" for rec in recommendations])\n\n    html_content = f\"\"\"\n    <!DOCTYPE html>\n    <html lang=\"en\">\n    <head>\n        <meta charset=\"UTF-8\">\n        <title>SEO border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; margin-bottom: 20px; }}\n            th, td {{ border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 8px; text-align: left; }}\n            th {{ background-color: #f2f2f2; }}\n            .severity-high {{ color: red; font-weight: bold; }}\n            .severity-medium {{ color: orange; font-weight: bold; }}\n            .severity-low {{ color: green; font-weight: bold; }}\n        </style>\n    </head>\n    <body>\n        <h1>SEO Audit Report: {site_name}</h1>\n        <p><strong>URLs Crawled:</strong> {urls_crawled}</p>\n\n        <h2>Issue Summary</h2>\n        <ul>\n            {severity_html}\n        </ul>\n\n        <h2>Top Issues</h2>\n        <table>\n            <thead>\n                <tr><th>Severity</th><th>Issue</th><th>URL</th></tr>\n            </thead>\n            <tbody>\n                {issues_rows}\n            </tbody>\n        </table>\n\n        <h2>Recommendations</h2>\n        <ul>\n            {recs_html}\n        </ul>\n    </body>\n    </html>\n    \"\"\"\n\n    with open(output_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(html_content)\n\ndef to_pdf(report_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Writes outputs/report.pdf using fpdf2 library.\n    \"\"\"\n    ensure_output_dir()\n    output_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"report.pdf\")\n\n    pdf = FPDF()\n    pdf.add_page()\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"B\", 16)\n\n    site_name = report_dict.get(\"site_name\", \"Unknown Site\")\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, f\"SEO Audit Report: {site_name}\", ln=True, align=\"C\")\n    pdf.ln(10)\n\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"\", 12)\n    urls_crawled = report_dict.get(\"urls_crawled\", 0)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, f\"URLs Crawled: {urls_crawled}\", ln=True)\n    pdf.ln(5)\n\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"B\", 14)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, \"Issue Summary\", ln=True)\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"\", 12)\n    severity_counts = report_dict.get(\"severity_counts\", {})\n    for sev, count in severity_counts.items():\n        pdf.cell(0, 10, f\"{sev}: {count}\", ln=True)\n    pdf.ln(5)\n\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"B\", 14)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, \"Top Issues\", ln=True)\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"\", 10)\n\n    # Simple table header\n    pdf.cell(30, 10, \"Severity\", 1)\n    pdf.cell(80, 10, \"Issue\", 1)\n    pdf.cell(80, 10, \"URL\", 1)\n    pdf.ln()\n\n    top_issues = report_dict.get(\"top_issues\", [])\n    for issue in top_issues:\n        pdf.cell(30, 10, issue.get(\"severity\", \"N/A\"), 1)\n        pdf.cell(80, 10, issue.get(\"issue\", \"N/A\")[:75], 1) # Simple truncation\n        pdf.cell(80, 10, issue.get(\"url\", \"N/A\")[:75], 1)\n        pdf.ln()\n    pdf.ln(5)\n\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"B\", 14)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, \"Recommendations\", ln=True)\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"\", 12)\n    recommendations = report_dict.get(\"recommendations\", [])\n    for rec in recommendations:\n        pdf.multi_cell(0, 10, f\"- {rec}\")\n        pdf.ln(2)\n\n    pdf.output(output_path)\n\ndef to_pptx(report_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Writes outputs/report.pptx using python-pptx library.\n    \"\"\"\n    ensure_output_dir()\n    output_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"report.pptx\")\n\n    prs = Presentation()\n\n    # Slide 1: Title\n    title_slide_layout = prs.slide_layouts[0]\n    slide = prs.slides.add_slide(title_slide_layout)\n    title = slide.shapes.title\n    subtitle = slide.placeholders[1]\n    site_name = report_dict.get(\"site_name\", \"Unknown Site\")\n    title.text = f\"SEO Audit Report\"\n    subtitle.text = f\"Site: {site_name}\\nGenerated on 2026-06-06\"\n\n    # Slide 2: Summary\n    summary_layout = prs.slide_layouts[1]\n    slide = prs.slides.add_slide(summary_layout)\n    slide.shapes.title.text = = report_dict.get(\"severity_counts\", {})\n    for sev, count in severity_counts.items():\n        p = tf.add_paragraph()\n        p.text = f\"{sev} Severity Issues: {count}\"\n\n    # Slide 3: Top top_issues = report_dict.get(\"top_issues\", [])\n    if not top_issues:\n        tf.text = \"No critical issues found.\"\n    else:\n        for issue in top_issues[:5]: # Limit to top 5 for slide report_dict.get(\"recommendations\", [])\n    if not recommendations:\n        tf.text = \"No specific recommendations at this time.\"\n    else:\n        for rec in recommendations[:5]: # Limit to Please run the pipeline first to generate <code>outputs/report.json</code>.</p>\n                </body>\n            </html>\n            \"\"\",\n            status_code=200\n        )\n\n    try:\n        with open(REPORT_PATH, \"r\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n            data = json.load(f)\n    except Exception as e:\n        return HTMLResponse(content=f\"Error reading report: {str(e)}\", status_code=500)\n\n    site_name = data.get(\"site_name\", \"Unknown Site\")\n    urls_crawled = data.get(\"urls_crawled\", 0)\n    severity_counts = data.get(\"severity_counts\", {})\n    top_issues = data.get(\"top_issues\", [])[:20]\n\n    total_issues = sum(severity_counts.values())\n\n    # Build issues table rows\n    table_rows = \"\"\n    for issue in top_issues:\n        table_rows += f\"\"\"\n        <tr>\n            <td>{issue.get('url', 'N/A')}</td>\n            <td>{issue.get('issue', 'N/A')}</td>\n            <td>{issue.get('severity', 'N/A')}</td>\n            <td>{issue.get('detail', 'N/A')}</td>\n        </tr>\n        \"\"\"\n\n    html_content = f\"\"\"\n    <html>\n        <head>\n            <title>SEO Dashboard - {site_name}</title>\n            <style>\n                body {{ font-family: 'Segoe UI', Tahoma, Geneva, Verdana, sans-serif; margin: 40px; background-color: #f4f7f6; }}\n                .container {{ max-width: 1200px; margin:               .stats-grid {{ display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(200px, 1fr)); gap: 20px; margin-bottom: 30px; }}\n                .stat-card {{ padding: 20px; background: #edf2f7; border-radius: 8px; text-align: center; }}\n                .stat-card h3 {{ margin: 0; color: #4a5568; font-size: 0.9rem; text-transform: uppercase; }}\n                .stat-card p {{ margin: 10px 0 0; font-size: 1.8rem; font-weight: bold; color: #2d3748; }}\n                table {{ width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-top: 20px; }}\n                th, td {{ padding: }}\n                .sev-high {{ color: #e53e3e; font-weight: bold; }}\n                .sev-medium {{ color: #dd6b20; font-weight: bold; }}\n                .sev-low {{ color: #38a169; font-weight: bold; }}\n            </style>\n        </head>\n        <body>\n            <div class=\"container\">\n                <h1>SEO Report: {site_name}</h1>\n\n                <div class=\"stats-grid\">\n                    <div class=\"stat-card\">\n                        <h3>URLs Crawled</h3>\n                        <p>{urls_crawled}</p>\n                    </div>\n                    <div               <h3>High Severity</h3>\n                        <p class=\"sev-high\">{severity_counts.get('High', 0)}</p>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"stat-card\">\n                        <h3>Medium Severity</h3>\n                        <p class=\"sev-medium\">{severity_counts.get('Medium', 0)}</p>\n                    </div>\n                    <div class=\"stat-card\">\n                        <h3>Low Severity</h3>\n                        <p class=\"sev-low\">{severity_counts.get('Low', 0)}</p>\n                    </div>\n collections import defaultdict\n\n# Severity Mapping from CLAUDE.md\nSEVERITY = {\n    \"high\": [\n        \"missing_title\", \"duplicate_title\", \"broken_link\",\n        \"server_error\", \"redirect_chain\", \"redirect_loop\"\n    ],\n    \"medium\": [\n        \"title_too_long\", \"missing_meta_description\", \"duplicate_meta_description\",\n        \"missing_h1\", \"redirect\", \"missing_image_alt\",\n        \"orphan_page\", \"non_indexable_but_linked\", \"canonical_mismatch\"\n    ],\n    \"low\": [\n        \"title_too_short\", \"meta_description_too_long\", \"duplicate_h1\",\n        \"thin_content\", \"slow_page\"\n    ]\n}\n\ndef get_severity(issue_type):\n    for sev, issues in SEVERITY.items():\n        if issue_type in issues:\n            return sev\n    return \"low\"\n\ndef load_csv(path: str) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Loads Screaming Frog internal_all.csv into a pandas DataFrame.\"\"\"\n    return pd.read_csv(path, encoding=\"utf-8-sig\", low_memory=False)\n\ndef detect(df: pd.DataFrame) -> list:\n    \"\"\"\n    Detects SEO issues based on the provided DataFrame.\n    Returns a list of dicts with keys: url, issue_type, severity, detail.\n    \"\"\"\n    issues = []\n\n    # Standard Screaming Frog internal_all export column names\n    col_url = 'Address'\n    col_title = 'Title 1'\n    col_meta = 'Meta Description 1'\n    col_status = 'Status Code'\n    col_h1 = 'H1-1'\n    col_index = 'Indexability'\n    col_canonical = 'Canonical'\n    col_words = 'Word Count'\n    col_time = 'Response Time'\n    col_inlinks = 'Inlinks'\n    col_alt = 'Img Alt Text'\n    col_chain = 'Redirect Chain'\n    col_loop = 'Redirect Loop'\n\n    # Pre-calculate duplicates for efficient lookup\n    titles = df[col_title].fillna('') if col_title in df.columns else pd.Series([], dtype=str)\n    metas = df[col_meta].fillna('') if col_meta in df.columns else pd.Series([], dtype=str)\n    h1s = df[col_h1].fillna('') if col_h1 in df.columns else pd.Series([], dtype=str)\n\n    dup_titles = set(titles[titles.duplicated()].unique())\n    dup_metas = set(metas[metas.duplicated()].unique())\n    dup_h1s = set(h1s[h1s.duplicated()].unique())\n\n    for _, row in df.iterrows():\n        url = row.get(col_url, \"Unknown\")\n        title = str(row.get(col_title, \"\")) if pd.notnull(row.get(col_title)) else \"\"\n        meta = str(row.get(col_meta, \"\")) if pd.notnull(row.get(col_meta)) else \"\"\n        status = row.get(col_status)\n        h1 = str(row.get(col_h1, \"\")) if pd.notnull(row.get(col_h1)) else \"\"\n        indexability = row.get(col_index)\n        canonical = str(row.get(col_canonical, \"\")) if pd.notnull(row.get(col_canonical)) else \"\"\n        words = row.get(col_words)\n        resp_time = row.get(col_time)\n        inlinks = row.get(col_inlinks)\n\n        # Rule definitions\n        checks = [\n            (\"missing_title\", not title, \"Page is missing a title tag\"),\n            (\"duplicate_title\", title and title in dup_titles, f\"Duplicate title: {title}\"),\n            (\"broken_link\", isinstance(status, (int, float)) and 400 <= status < 500, f\"Broken link with status {status}\"),\n            (\"server_error\", isinstance(status, (int, float)) and status >= 500, f\"Server error with status {status}\"),\n            (\"redirect_chain\", row.get(col_chain) if col_chain in df.columns else False, \"Page is part of a redirect chain\"),\n            (\"redirect_loop\", row.get(col_loop) if col_loop in df.columns else False, \"Page is in a redirect loop\"),\n            (\"title_too_long\", len(title) > 60, f\"Title length ({len(title)}) exceeds 60 characters\"),\n            (\"missing_meta_description\", not meta, \"Meta description is missing\"),\n            (\"duplicate_meta_description\", meta and meta in dup_metas, \"Duplicate meta description detected\"),\n            (\"missing_h1\", not h1, \"H1 tag is missing\"),\n            (\"redirect\", isinstance(status, (int, float)) and status in [301, 302], f\"Page is a redirect ({status})\"),\n            (\"missing_image_alt\", (col_alt in df.columns and (pd.isnull(row.get(col_alt)) or not str(row.get(col_alt)).strip())), \"Image missing alt text\"),\n            (\"orphan_page\", isinstance(inlinks, (int, float)) and inlinks == 0, \"Page has 0 inlinks (orphan page)\"),\n            (\"non_indexable_but_linked\", indexability != \"Indexable\", f\"Page is non-indexable ({indexability}) but present in crawl\"),\n            (\"canonical_mismatch\", canonical and canonical != url, f\"Canonical mismatch: {canonical}\"),\n            (\"title_too_short\", title and len(title) < 30, f\"Title length ({len(title)}) is too short (below 30)\"),\n            (\"meta_description_too_long\", meta and len(meta) > 155, f\"Meta description length ({len(meta)}) exceeds 155 characters\"),\n            (\"duplicate_h1\", h1 and h1 in dup_h1s, f\"Duplicate H1 detected: {h1}\"),\n            (\"thin_content\", isinstance(words, (int, float)) and words < 300, f\"Thin content: only {words} words\"),\n            (\"slow_page\", isinstance(resp_time, (int, float)) and resp_time > 2000, f\"Page response time is slow: {resp_time}ms\"),\n        ]\n\n        for issue_type, condition, detail in checks:\n            if condition:\n                issues.append({\n                    \"url\": url,\n                    \"issue_type\": issue_type,\n                    \"severity\": get_severity(issue_type),\n                    \"detail\": detail\n                })\n\n    return issues\n\ndef summarize(issues: list) -> dict:\n    by_sev = defaultdict(int)\n    for i in issues:\n        by_sev[i[\"severity\"].capitalize()] += 1\n    return {\n        \"total_issues\": len(issues),\n        \"by_severity\": {\n            \"High\": by_sev[\"High\"],\n            \"Medium\": by_sev[\"Medium\"],\n            \"Low\": by_sev[\"Low\"]\n        }\n    }\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":48,"oldLines":6,"newStart":48,"newLines":7,"lines":["     col_time = 'Response \"\"\"Loads Screaming Frog internal_all.csv into a pandas DataFrame.\"\"\"\n    return pd.read_csv(path, encoding=\"utf-8-sig\", low_memory=False)\n\ndef detect(df: pd.DataFrame) -> list:\n    col_canonical = 'Canonical Link Element 1'","originalFile":"import pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\n# Severity Mapping from CLAUDE.md\nSEVERITY = {\n    \"high\": [\n        \"missing_title\", \"duplicate_title\", \"broken_link\",\n        \"server_error\", \"redirect_chain\", \"redirect_loop\"\n    ],\n    \"medium\": [\n        \"title_too_long\", \"missing_meta_description\", \"duplicate_meta_description\",\n        \"missing_h1\", \"redirect\", \"missing_image_alt\",\n        \"orphan_page\", \"non_indexable_but_linked\", \"canonical_mismatch\"\n    ],\n    \"low\": [\n        \"title_too_short\", \"meta_description_too_long\", \"duplicate_h1\",\n        \"thin_content\", \"slow_page\"\n    ]\n}\n\ndef get_severity(issue_type):\n    for sev, issues in SEVERITY.items():\n        if issue_type in issues:\n            return sev\n    return \"low\"\n\ndef load_csv(path: str) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Loads Screaming Frog internal_all.csv into a pandas DataFrame.\"\"\"\n    return pd.read_csv(path, encoding=\"utf-8-sig\", low_memory=False)\n\ndef detect(df: pd.DataFrame) -> list:\n    \"\"\"\n    Detects SEO issues based on the provided DataFrame.\n    Returns a list of dicts with keys: url, issue_type, severity, detail.\n    \"\"\"\n    issues = []\n\n    # Standard Screaming Frog internal_all export column names\n    col_url = 'Address'\n    col_title = 'Title 1'\n    col_meta = 'Meta Description 1'\n    col_status = 'Status Code'\n    col_h1 = 'H1-1'\n    col_index = 'Indexability'\n    col_canonical = 'Canonical'\n    col_words = 'Word Count'\n    col_time = 'Response Time'\n    col_inlinks = 'Inlinks'\n    col_alt = 'Img Alt Text'\n    col_content_type = 'Content Type'\n    col_chain = 'Redirect Chain'\n    col_loop = 'Redirect Loop'\n\n    # Pre-calculate duplicates for efficient lookup\n    titles = df[col_title].fillna('') if col_title in df.columns else pd.Series([], dtype=str)\n    metas = df[col_meta].fillna('') if col_meta in df.columns else pd.Series([], dtype=str)\n    h1s = df[col_h1].fillna('') if col_h1 in df.columns else pd.Series([], dtype=str)\n\n    dup_titles = set(titles[titles.duplicated()].unique())\n    dup_metas = set(metas[metas.duplicated()].unique())\n    dup_h1s = set(h1s[h1s.duplicated()].unique())\n\n    for _, row in df.iterrows():\n        url = row.get(col_url, \"Unknown\")\n        title = str(row.get(col_title, \"\")) if pd.notnull(row.get(col_title)) else \"\"\n        meta = str(row.get(col_meta, \"\")) if pd.notnull(row.get(col_meta)) else \"\"\n        status = row.get(col_status)\n        h1 = str(row.get(col_h1, \"\")) if pd.notnull(row.get(col_h1)) else \"\"\n        indexability = row.get(col_index)\n        canonical = str(row.get(col_canonical, \"\")) if pd.notnull(row.get(col_canonical)) else \"\"\n        words = row.get(col_words)\n        resp_time = row.get(col_time)\n        inlinks = row.get(col_inlinks)\n\n        # Rule definitions\n        checks = [\n            (\"missing_title\", (indexability == 'Indexable' and status == 200) and not title, \"Page is missing a title tag\"),\n            (\"duplicate_title\", (indexability == 'Indexable' and status == 200) and (title and title in dup_titles), f\"Duplicate title: {title}\"),\n            (\"broken_link\", isinstance(status, (int, float)) and 400 <= status < 500, f\"Broken link with status {status}\"),\n            (\"server_error\", isinstance(status, (int, float)) and status >= 500, f\"Server error with status {status}\"),\n            (\"redirect_chain\", row.get(col_chain) if col_chain in df.columns else False, \"Page is part of a redirect chain\"),\n            (\"redirect_loop\", row.get(col_loop) if col_loop in df.columns else False, \"Page is in a redirect loop\"),\n            (\"title_too_long\", len(title) > 60, f\"Title length ({len(title)}) exceeds 60 characters\"),\n            (\"missing_meta_description\", (indexability == 'Indexable' and status == 200) and not meta, \"Meta description is missing\"),\n            (\"duplicate_meta_description\", (indexability == 'Indexable' and status == 200) and (meta and meta in dup_metas), \"Duplicate meta description detected\"),\n            (\"missing_h1\", not h1, \"H1 tag is missing\"),\n            (\"redirect\", isinstance(status, (int, float)) and status in [301, 302], f\"Page is a redirect ({status})\"),\n            (\"missing_image_alt\", (str(row.get('Content Type', '')).lower().find('image') != -1) and not title, \"Image missing alt text\"),\n            (\"orphan_page\", status == 200 and isinstance(inlinks, (int, float)) and inlinks == 0, \"Page has 0 inlinks (orphan page)\"),\n            (\"non_indexable_but_linked\", status == 200 and indexability != \"Indexable\", f\"Page is non-indexable ({indexability}) but present in crawl\"),\n            (\"canonical_mismatch\", canonical and canonical != url, f\"Canonical mismatch: {canonical}\"),\n            (\"title_too_short\", title and len(title) < 30, f\"Title length ({len(title)}) is too short (below 30)\"),\n            (\"meta_description_too_long\", meta and len(meta) > 155, f\"Meta description length ({len(meta)}) exceeds 155 characters\"),\n            (\"duplicate_h1\", h1 and h1 in dup_h1s, f\"Duplicate H1 detected: {h1}\"),\n            (\"thin_content\", isinstance(words, (int, float)) and words < 200, f\"Thin content: only {words} words\"),\n            (\"slow_page\", isinstance(resp_time, (int, float)) and resp_time > 1000, f\"Page response time is slow: {resp_time}ms\"),\n        ]\n\n        for issue_type, condition, detail in checks:\n            if condition:\n                issues.append({\n                    \"url\": url,\n                    \"issue_type\": issue_type,\n                    \"severity\": get_severity(issue_type),\n                    \"detail\": detail\n                })\n\n    return issues\n\ndef summarize(issues: list) -> dict:\n    by_sev = defaultdict(int)\n    for i in issues:\n        by_sev[i[\"severity\"].capitalize()] += 1\n    return {\n        \"total_issues\": len(issues),\n        \"by_severity\": {\n            \"High\": by_sev[\"High\"],\n            \"Medium\": by_sev[\"Medium\"],\n            \"Low\": by_sev[\"Low\"]\n        }\n    }\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":43,"oldLines":7,"newStart":43,"newLines":7,"lines":["     col_status = 'Status set(h1s[h1s.duplicated()].unique())","originalFile":"import pandas as pd\nimport numpy as np\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\n# Severity Mapping from CLAUDE.md\nSEVERITY = {\n    \"high\": [\n        \"missing_title\", \"duplicate_title\", \"broken_link\",\n        \"server_error\", \"redirect_chain\", \"redirect_loop\"\n    ],\n    \"medium\": [\n        \"title_too_long\", \"missing_meta_description\", \"duplicate_meta_description\",\n        \"missing_h1\", \"redirect\", \"missing_image_alt\",\n        \"orphan_page\", \"non_indexable_but_linked\", \"canonical_mismatch\"\n    ],\n    \"low\": [\n        \"title_too_short\", \"meta_description_too_long\", \"duplicate_h1\",\n        \"thin_content\", \"slow_page\"\n    ]\n}\n\ndef get_severity(issue_type):\n    for sev, issues in SEVERITY.items():\n        if issue_type in issues:\n            return sev\n    return \"low\"\n\ndef load_csv(path: str) -> pd.DataFrame:\n    \"\"\"Loads Screaming Frog internal_all.csv into a pandas DataFrame.\"\"\"\n    return pd.read_csv(path, encoding=\"utf-8-sig\", low_memory=False)\n\ndef detect(df: pd.DataFrame) -> list:\n    \"\"\"\n    Detects SEO issues based on the provided DataFrame.\n    Returns a list of dicts with keys: url, issue_type, severity, detail.\n    \"\"\"\n    issues = []\n\n    # Standard Screaming Frog internal_all export column names\n    col_url = 'Address'\n    col_title = 'Title 1'\n    col_meta = 'Meta Description 1'\n    col_status = 'Status Code'\n    col_h1 = 'H1-1'\n    col_index = 'Indexability'\n    col_canonical = 'Canonical Link Element 1'\n    col_words = 'Word Count'\n    col_time = 'Response Time'\n    col_inlinks = 'Inlinks'\n    col_alt = 'Img Alt Text'\n    col_content_type = 'Content Type'\n    col_chain = 'Redirect Chain'\n    col_loop = 'Redirect Loop'\n\n    # Pre-calculate duplicates for efficient lookup\n    titles = df[col_title].fillna('') if col_title in df.columns else pd.Series([], dtype=str)\n    metas = df[col_meta].fillna('') if col_meta in df.columns else pd.Series([], dtype=str)\n    h1s = df[col_h1].fillna('') if col_h1 in df.columns else pd.Series([], dtype=str)\n\n    dup_titles = set(titles[titles.duplicated()].unique())\n    dup_metas = set(metas[metas.duplicated()].unique())\n    dup_h1s = set(h1s[h1s.duplicated()].unique())\n\n    for _, row in df.iterrows():\n        url = row.get(col_url, \"Unknown\")\n        title = str(row.get(col_title, \"\")) if pd.notnull(row.get(col_title)) else \"\"\n        meta = str(row.get(col_meta, \"\")) if pd.notnull(row.get(col_meta)) else \"\"\n        status = row.get(col_status)\n        h1 = str(row.get(col_h1, \"\")) if pd.notnull(row.get(col_h1)) else \"\"\n        indexability = row.get(col_index)\n        canonical = str(row.get(col_canonical, \"\")) if pd.notnull(row.get(col_canonical)) else \"\"\n        words = row.get(col_words)\n        resp_time = row.get(col_time)\n        inlinks = row.get(col_inlinks)\n\n        # Rule definitions\n        checks = [\n            (\"missing_title\", (indexability == 'Indexable' and status == 200) and not title, \"Page is missing a title tag\"),\n            (\"duplicate_title\", (indexability == 'Indexable' and status == 200) and (title and title in dup_titles), f\"Duplicate title: {title}\"),\n            (\"broken_link\", isinstance(status, (int, float)) and 400 <= status < 500, f\"Broken link with status {status}\"),\n            (\"server_error\", isinstance(status, (int, float)) and status >= 500, f\"Server error with status {status}\"),\n            (\"redirect_chain\", row.get(col_chain) if col_chain in df.columns else False, \"Page is part of a redirect chain\"),\n            (\"redirect_loop\", row.get(col_loop) if col_loop in df.columns else False, \"Page is in a redirect loop\"),\n            (\"title_too_long\", len(title) > 60, f\"Title length ({len(title)}) exceeds 60 characters\"),\n            (\"missing_meta_description\", (indexability == 'Indexable' and status == 200) and not meta, \"Meta description is missing\"),\n            (\"duplicate_meta_description\", (indexability == 'Indexable' and status == 200) and (meta and meta in dup_metas), \"Duplicate meta description detected\"),\n            (\"missing_h1\", not h1, \"H1 tag is missing\"),\n            (\"redirect\", isinstance(status, (int, float)) and status in [301, 302], f\"Page is a redirect ({status})\"),\n            (\"missing_image_alt\", (str(row.get('Content Type', '')).lower().find('image') != -1) and not title, \"Image missing alt text\"),\n            (\"orphan_page\", status == 200 and isinstance(inlinks, (int, float)) and inlinks == 0, \"Page has 0 inlinks (orphan page)\"),\n            (\"non_indexable_but_linked\", status == 200 and indexability != \"Indexable\", f\"Page is non-indexable ({indexability}) but present in crawl\"),\n            (\"canonical_mismatch\", canonical and canonical != url, f\"Canonical mismatch: {canonical}\"),\n            (\"title_too_short\", title and len(title) < 30, f\"Title length ({len(title)}) is too short (below 30)\"),\n            (\"meta_description_too_long\", meta and len(meta) > 155, f\"Meta description length ({len(meta)}) exceeds 155 characters\"),\n            (\"duplicate_h1\", h1 and h1 in dup_h1s, f\"Duplicate H1 detected: {h1}\"),\n            (\"thin_content\", isinstance(words, (int, float)) and words < 200, f\"Thin content: only {words} words\"),\n            (\"slow_page\", isinstance(resp_time, (int, float)) and resp_time > 1.0, f\"Page response time is slow: {resp_time}ms\"),\n        ]\n\n        for issue_type, condition, detail in checks:\n            if condition:\n                issues.append({\n                    \"url\": url,\n                    \"issue_type\": issue_type,\n                    \"severity\": get_severity(issue_type),\n                    \"detail\": detail\n                })\n\n    return issues\n\ndef summarize(issues: list) -> dict:\n    by_sev = defaultdict(int)\n    for i in issues:\n        by_sev[i[\"severity\"].capitalize()] += 1\n    return {\n        \"total_issues\": len(issues),\n        \"by_severity\": {\n            \"High\": by_sev[\"High\"],\n            \"Medium\": by_sev[\"Medium\"],\n            \"Low\": by_sev[\"Low\"]\n        }\n    }\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":53,"oldLines":9,"newStart":53,"newLines":10,"lines":["     col_loop = {"parentUuid":"947453cb-1dab-401f-b545-1cd799f57444","isSidechain":false,"message":{"id":"msg_680ed9cc6aceab2a4399f11c","type":"message","role":"assistant","model":"gemma4:31b","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"call_99httbh6","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"seo/fixer.py","content":"import requests\nimport json\n\n_call_count = 0\nOLLAMA_URL = \"http://localhost:11434/api/generate\"\nMODEL = \"gemma4:31b-cloud\"\n\ndef _ask(prompt):\n    global _call_count\n    try:\n        response = requests.post(\n            OLLAMA_URL,\n            json={\n                \"model\": MODEL,\n                \"prompt\": prompt,\n                \"stream\": False\n            },\n            timeout=30\n        )\n        response.raise_for_status()\n        _call_count += 1\n        return response.json().get(\"response\", \"\").strip()\n    except Exception:\n        return \"\"\n\ndef rewrite_title(url, old_title):\n    prompt = f\"Rewrite this SEO title for the page {url}. pathlib import Path\nfrom fpdf import FPDF\nfrom pptx import Presentation\n\ndef to_html(report: dict, path: str):\n    site = report.get(\"site\", \"Unknown Site\")\n    urls_crawled = report.get(\"recommendations\", [])\n\n    severity_html = \"\".join([f\"<li>{sev}: {count}</li>\" for sev, count in severity_counts.items()])\n\n    issues_rows = \"\"\n    for issue in issues:\n        issues_rows += f\"\"\"\n        <tr>\n            <td>{issue.get('severity', 'N/A')}</td>\n            <td>{issue.get('type', 'N/A')}</td>\n            <td>{issue.get('count', 0)}</td>\n  th {{ background-color: #f2f2f2; }}\n        </style>\n    </head>\n    <body>\n        <h1>SEO Audit Report: {site}</h1>\n        <p><strong>URLs Crawled:</strong> {urls_crawled}</p>\n        <h2>Issue Summary</h2>\n        <ul>{severity_html}</ul>\n        <h2>Issues</h2>\n        <table>\n            <thead><tr><th>Severity</th><th>Type</th><th>Count</th><th>Explanation</th></tr></thead>\n            <tbody>{issues_rows}</tbody>\n        </table>\n        <h2>Recommendations</h2>\n        <ul>{recs_html}</ul>\n    </body>\n    </html>\n    \"\"\"\n    Path(path).write_text(html_content, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n\ndef to_pdf(report: dict, path: str):\n    site = report.get(\"site\", \"Unknown Site\")\n    urls_crawled = report.get(\"urls_crawled\", 0)\n    summary = report.get(\"summary\", {})\n    by_severity = summary.get(\"by_severity\", {})\n    issues = report.get(\"issues\", [])\n    recommendations = report.get(\"recommendations\", [])\n    total_issues = sum(by_severity.values())\n\n    pdf = FPDF()\n    pdf.add_page()\n    pdf.set_font(\"Helvetica\", \"B\", 16)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, f\"SEO Audit Report: {site}\", ln=True, align=\"C\")\n    pdf.ln(5)\n\n    pdf.set_font(\"Helvetica\", \"\", 12)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, f\"URLs Crawled: {urls_crawled}\", ln=True)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, f\"Total Issues: {total_issues}\", ln=True)\n    pdf.ln(10)\n\n    pdf.set_font(\"Helvetica\", \"B\", 14)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, \"Issues\", ln=True)\n    pdf.set_font(\"Helvetica\", \"\", 12)\n    for issue in issues:\n        text = f\"[{issue.get('severity', 'N/A')}] {issue.get('type', 'N/A')} - {issue.get('count', 0)} URLs\"\n        pdf.cell(0, 10, text, ln=True)\n\n    pdf.ln(10)\n    pdf.set_font(\"Helvetica\", \"B\", 14)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, \"Recommendations\", ln=True)\n    pdf.set_font(\"Helvetica\", \"\", 12)\n    for rec in recommendations:\n        pdf.multi_cell(0, 10, f\"- {rec}\")\n        pdf.ln(2)\n\n    pdf.output(path)\n\ndef to_pptx(report: dict, path: str):\n    site = report.get(\"site\", \"Unknown Site\")\n    by_severity = report.get(\"summary\", {}).get(\"by_severity\", {})\n    issues = report.get(\"issues\", [])\n    recommendations = report.get(\"recommendations\", [])\n\n    prs = Presentation()\n\n    # Slide 1: Title\n    slide = prs.slides.add_slide(prs.slide_layouts[0])\n    slide.shapes.title.text = \"SEO Audit Report\"\n    slide.placeholders[1].text = f\"Site: {site}\"\n\n    # Slide 2: Summary\n    slide = prs.slides.add_slide(prs.slide_layouts[1])\n    slide.shapes.title.text = \"Summary\"\n    tf = slide.placeholders[1].text_frame\n    for sev, count in by_severity.items():\n        p = tf.add_paragraph()\n        p.text = f\"{sev}: {count}\"\n\n    # Slide 3: Top Issues\n    slide = prs.slides.add_slide(prs.slide_layouts[1])\n    slide.shapes.title.text = \"Top Issues\"\n    tf = slide.placeholders[1].text_frame\n    for issue in issues[:8]:\n        p = tf.add_paragraph()\n        p.text = f\"[{issue.get('severity', 'N/A')}] 0","+    for rec in recommendations:","+        p = tf.add_paragraph()","+        p.text = rec"," ","-    prs.save(output_path)","+    prs.save(path)"]}],"originalFile":"import os\nfrom fpdf import FPDF\nfrom pptx import Presentation\nfrom pptx.util import Inches, Pt\n\nOUTPUT_DIR = \"outputs\"\n\ndef ensure_output_dir():\n    if not os.path.exists(OUTPUT_DIR):\n        os.makedirs(OUTPUT_DIR)\n\ndef to_html(report_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Writes outputs/report.html showing: site name, urls crawled,\n    issue counts by severity, top issues as an HTML table, recommendations list.\n    \"\"\"\n    ensure_output_dir()\n    output_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"report.html\")\n\n    site_name = report_dict.get(\"site_name\", \"Unknown Site\")\n    urls_crawled = report_dict.get(\"urls_crawled\", 0)\n    severity_counts = report_dict.get(\"severity_counts\", {})\n    top_issues = report_dict.get(\"top_issues\", [])\n    recommendations = report_dict.get(\"recommendations\", [])\n\n    severity_html = \"\".join([f\"<li>{sev}: {count}</li>\" for sev, count in severity_counts.items()])\n\n    issues_rows = \"\".join([\n        f\"<tr><td>{issue.get('severity', 'N/A')}</td><td>{issue.get('issue', 'N/A')}</td><td>{issue.get('url', 'N/A')}</td></tr>\"\n        for issue in top_issues\n    ])\n\n    recs_html = \"\".join([f\"<li>{rec}</li>\" for rec in recommendations])\n\n    html_content = f\"\"\"\n    <!DOCTYPE html>\n    <html lang=\"en\">\n    <head>\n        <meta charset=\"UTF-8\">\n        <title>SEO Report - {site_name}</title>\n        <style>\n            body {{ font-family: Arial, sans-serif; margin: 40px; line-height: 1.6; }}\n            h1, h2 {{ color: #333; }}\n            table {{ border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; margin-bottom: 20px; }}\n            th, td {{ border: 1px solid #ddd; padding: 8px; text-align: left; }}\n            th {{ background-color: #f2f2f2; }}\n            .severity-high {{ color: red; font-weight: bold; }}\n            .severity-medium {{ color: orange; font-weight: bold; }}\n            .severity-low {{ color: green; font-weight: bold; }}\n        </style>\n    </head>\n    <body>\n        <h1>SEO Audit Report: {site_name}</h1>\n        <p><strong>URLs Crawled:</strong> {urls_crawled}</p>\n\n        <h2>Issue Summary</h2>\n        <ul>\n            {severity_html}\n        </ul>\n\n        <h2>Top Issues</h2>\n        <table>\n            <thead>\n                <tr><th>Severity</th><th>Issue</th><th>URL</th></tr>\n            </thead>\n            <tbody>\n                {issues_rows}\n            </tbody>\n        </table>\n\n        <h2>Recommendations</h2>\n        <ul>\n            {recs_html}\n        </ul>\n    </body>\n    </html>\n    \"\"\"\n\n    with open(output_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n        f.write(html_content)\n\ndef to_pdf(report_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Writes outputs/report.pdf using fpdf2 library.\n    \"\"\"\n    ensure_output_dir()\n    output_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"report.pdf\")\n\n    pdf = FPDF()\n    pdf.add_page()\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"B\", 16)\n\n    site_name = report_dict.get(\"site_name\", \"Unknown Site\")\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, f\"SEO Audit Report: {site_name}\", ln=True, align=\"C\")\n    pdf.ln(10)\n\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"\", 12)\n    urls_crawled = report_dict.get(\"urls_crawled\", 0)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, f\"URLs Crawled: {urls_crawled}\", ln=True)\n    pdf.ln(5)\n\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"B\", 14)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, \"Issue Summary\", ln=True)\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"\", 12)\n    severity_counts = report_dict.get(\"severity_counts\", {})\n    for sev, count in severity_counts.items():\n        pdf.cell(0, 10, f\"{sev}: {count}\", ln=True)\n    pdf.ln(5)\n\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"B\", 14)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, \"Top Issues\", ln=True)\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"\", 10)\n\n    # Simple table header\n    pdf.cell(30, 10, \"Severity\", 1)\n    pdf.cell(80, 10, \"Issue\", 1)\n    pdf.cell(80, 10, \"URL\", 1)\n    pdf.ln()\n\n    top_issues = report_dict.get(\"top_issues\", [])\n    for issue in top_issues:\n        pdf.cell(30, 10, issue.get(\"severity\", \"N/A\"), 1)\n        pdf.cell(80, 10, issue.get(\"issue\", \"N/A\")[:75], 1) # Simple truncation\n        pdf.cell(80, 10, issue.get(\"url\", \"N/A\")[:75], 1)\n        pdf.ln()\n    pdf.ln(5)\n\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"B\", 14)\n    pdf.cell(0, 10, \"Recommendations\", ln=True)\n    pdf.set_font(\"Arial\", \"\", 12)\n    recommendations = report_dict.get(\"recommendations\", [])\n    for rec in recommendations:\n        pdf.multi_cell(0, 10, f\"- {rec}\")\n        pdf.ln(2)\n\n    pdf.output(output_path)\n\ndef to_pptx(report_dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    Writes outputs/report.pptx using python-pptx library.\n    \"\"\"\n    ensure_output_dir()\n    output_path = os.path.join(OUTPUT_DIR, \"report.pptx\")\n\n    prs = Presentation()\n\n    # Slide 1: Title\n    title_slide_layout = prs.slide_layouts[0]\n    slide = prs.slides.add_slide(title_slide_layout)\n    title = slide.shapes.title\n    subtitle = slide.placeholders[1]\n    site_name = report_dict.get(\"site_name\", \"Unknown Site\")\n    title.text = f\"SEO Audit Report\"\n    subtitle.text = f\"Site: {site_name}\\nGenerated on 2026-06-06\"\n\n    # Slide 2: Summary\n    summary_layout = prs.slide_layouts[1]\n    slide = prs.slides.add_slide(summary_layout)\n    slide.shapes.title.text = \"Executive Summary\"\n    tf = slide.placeholders[1].text_frame\n    urls_crawled = report_dict.get(\"urls_crawled\", 0)\n    tf.text = f\"Total URLs Crawled: {urls_crawled}\"\n\n    severity_counts = report_dict.get(\"severity_counts\", {})\n    for sev, count in severity_counts.items():\n        p = tf.add_paragraph()\n        p.text = f\"{sev} Severity Issues: {count}\"\n\n    # Slide 3: Top Issues\n    issues_layout = prs.slide_layouts[1]\n    slide = prs.slides.add_slide(issues_layout)\n    slide.shapes.title.text = \"Top SEO Issues\"\n    tf = slide.placeholders[1].text_frame\n    top_issues = report_dict.get(\"top_issues\", [])\n    if not top_issues:\n        tf.text = \"No critical issues found.\"\n    else:\n        for issue in top_issues[:5]: # Limit to top 5 for slide readability\n            p = tf.add_paragraph()\n            p.text = f\"[{issue.get('severity')}] {issue.get('issue')}\"\n            p.level = 0\n\n    # Slide 4: Recommendations\n    recs_layout = prs.slide_layouts[1]\n    slide = prs.slides.add_slide(recs_layout)\n    slide.shapes.title.text = \"Key Recommendations\"\n    tf = slide.placeholders[1].text_frame\n    recommendations = report_dict.get(\"recommendations\", [])\n    if not recommendations:\n        tf.text = \"No specific recommendations at this time.\"\n    else:\n        for rec in recommendations[:5]: # Limit to top 5\n            p = tf.add_paragraph()\n            p.text = rec\n            p.level = 0\n\n    prs.save(output_path)\n","userModified":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"52d05ffb-9d9d-4eba-888d-64a11c09c84c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/AayushShukla/Desktop/seo-command-center","sessionId":"29d97f88-2ce2-4ab7-b467-a84aae7f9c50","version":"2.1.167","gitBranch":"main"}

Let's write:\n\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom collections import defaultdict\n\ndef detect(df: pd.DataFrame) -> list:\n    issues = []\n    # Assume columns: 'URL', 'Title 1', 'Indexability', 'Status Code', 'Content Type', 'Meta Description 1'\n    # Helper to check if URL is indexable and status 200 and content type contains html\n    def is_indexable_html(row):\n        return (row.get('Indexability') == 'Indexable' and\n                str(row.get('Status Code')) == '200' and\n                'text/html' in str(row.get('Content Type', '')))\n    \n    # Group by Title 1\n    title_groups = df.groupby('Title 1')\n    # missing_title\n    missing_title = title_groups.apply(lambda g: g.iloc[0] if pd.notna(g['Title 1'].iloc[0]) else None)\n    # Actually we need to find rows where Title 1 is empty and conditions hold\n    missing_title_rows = df[(df['Title 1'].astype(str).str.strip() == '') & df.apply(is_indexable_html, axis=1)]\n    if not missing_title_rows.empty:\n        issues.append({\n            \"type\": \"missing_title\",\n            \"severity\": \"High\",\n            \"affected_urls\": missing_title_rows['URL'].tolist(),\n            \"count\": len(missing_title_rows),\n            \"explanation\": \"Title 1 is empty despite indexable, 200 HTML pages.\"\n        })\n    \n    # duplicate_title\n    dup_title_rows = df[(df['Title 1'].notna()) & (df['Title 1'].astype(str).str.strip() != '') &\n                        df.apply(is_indexable_html, axis=1)]\n    # Count occurrences of each Title 1\n    title_counts = dup_title_rows['Title 1'].value_counts()\n    dup_titles = title_counts[title_counts >= 2]\n    for title, cnt in dup_titles.items():\n        urls = dup_title_rows[dup_title_rows['Title 1'] == title]['URL'].tolist()\n        issues.append({\n            \"type\": \"duplicate_title\",\n            \"severity\": \"High\",\n            \"affected_urls\": urls,\n            \"count\": cnt,\n            \"explanation\": f\"Duplicate Title 1 '{title}' found on {cnt} indexable pages.\"\n        })\n    \n    # title_too_long\n    # Pixel Width > 561 or Title 1 Length > 60\n    # Assume 'Title 1 Pixel Width' column? Or we can approximate length > 60\n    title_len_condition = dup_title_rows['Title 1'].astype(str).str.len() > 60\n    # Also maybe width condition not present, so just length\n    long_title_rows = dup_title_rows[title_len_condition]\n    if not long_title_rows.empty:\n        issues.append({\n            \"type\": \"title_too_long\",\n            \"severity\": \"Medium\",\n            \"affected_urls\": long_title_rows['URL'].tolist(),\n            \"count\": len(long_title_rows),\n            \"explanation\": \"Title 1 length exceeds 60 characters or pixel width > 561.\"\n        })\n    \n    #
